# Third-party notices

The Galley binary is MIT (LICENSE) and is built with the crates and syntax grammars below, under their own licences. GTK, Pango, Cairo, GLib and gdk-pixbuf are linked dynamically from the system and are not distributed with it. Generated by `scripts/third-party`; the gate checks it is current.

## Syntax grammars

From bat 0.26.1 through two-face 0.5.2; the grammars left out are listed, with why, in crates/app/grammars-excluded.txt.

Most of the code for generating both theme and syntax dumps along with the
curation of said themes and syntaxes is taken from the
[`bat` project](https://github.com/sharkdp/bat).

## Licences two-face's listing lacks

bat's listing, which two-face carries, finds a grammar's licence only in a file named
`LICENSE`. These two are MIT, one in a `LICENCE` file and one in its README, at the commits bat
0.26.1 ships.
